# Agents · P · part 26

957 entries, pr-reviewer to prd-creator. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **pr-reviewer**: Adversarial code review for a Kamboocha pull request. Reads the PR diff through the GitHub MCP, checks it against the feature or bug skill and the repo's conventions, and posts a review (comments + verdict). Read and comment only — never merges, never edits code. Invoked by the /feature or /bug orchestrator; never talks to the user directly. ([kartik912/Kamboocha](https://github.com/kartik912/Kamboocha/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: Reviews a GitHub or Bitbucket Cloud pull request, or a local branch/worktree before a PR exists, against its linked GitHub/Jira ticket and acceptance criteria, the project's code conventions and best practices, YAGNI and DRY, test inclusion, and coverage regression (from CI). Reproduces or proves every finding before reporting it as a bug or requested change. Shows the review in chat; never edits PR code and posts to GitHub/Bitbucket only when explicitly told to. Use when given a PR number or URL, or a branch/worktree to review locally (e.g. by implementer). ([kcayme/k-agents](https://github.com/kcayme/k-agents/blob/HEAD/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: Use to review a pull request as a shippable unit. Checks correctness of the diff, completeness against its PRD and tickets, blast radius, test adequacy, ledger conformance and diff hygiene. The merge gate. Complements the review squad (correctness-reviewer, perf-memory-reviewer, reuse-reviewer), which goes deeper on one dimension each. Read-only; reports findings and a verdict, does not fix. ([KenKor/privacy-guard](https://github.com/KenKor/privacy-guard/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: Reviews code changes for quality, security, and best practices. Use PROACTIVELY after any code modification, before commits, and during PR reviews. ([kennyolofsson23-netizen/claude-code-config](https://github.com/kennyolofsson23-netizen/claude-code-config/blob/HEAD/agents/pr-reviewer.md))

- **pr-reviewer**: Review PR diff for diagnosis gaps. Use after CC closes a feature/fix, before opening PR for human review. ([levismadar80-ship-it/FoodMamkor](https://github.com/levismadar80-ship-it/FoodMamkor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pr-reviewer.md))
- **pr-reviewer**: One council reviewer for Margot. Given exactly one card name and a pull request's facts, it reads that card from its own pr-council skill, fetches what the card names at the head sha, judges the diff against that card's standard under a refute posture, cites every finding at file:line, and returns that card's findings with a Checked block recording what it examined. From round two it reviews the delta since the last reviewed head and closes its own card's standing findings. It authors nothing, emits no verdict, and never sees another reviewer's findings. Spawned once per card by the council d… ([lexijamesesq/publish-skills](https://github.com/lexijamesesq/publish-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/pr-reviewer.md))

- **pr-reviewer**: Use when a pull request needs the internal review (no external result on its head and merge cannot accept a self-check, a critical ticket, a high one whose risky part that result did not cover, or the owner asks), or for a diff or plan task without a PR, or a repo audit. - **pr-reviewer**: Read-only, separate-context general code reviewer. It reviews a changed diff for correctness, regressions, maintainability, test coverage, and API/contract hygiene, and emits categorized findings (Block / Risk / Nit) for the operator to act on. Use it when an atom changes code and you want a fresh review pass the implementing context lacks. This is the GENERAL code lens — NOT the security lens (auth / secrets / supply-chain is the separate security-reviewer agent). Advisory assistant + mistake-catcher for the trusted operator — not an authority that approves or merges. ([lukasrepublic/agentic-foundry](https://github.com/lukasrepublic/agentic-foundry/blob/HEAD/agents/pr-reviewer.md))

- **pragmatist**: Use for code reviews, proposal audits, and design reviews where you want a "least change, ship fastest, lowest risk" lens applied before committing to an approach. Pragmatist actively resists over-engineering, names simpler alternatives with file:line evidence, and measures the scope of proposed changes. Trigger phrases: "pragmatist review", "least-change alternative", "is this worth the scope", "audit this proposal for over-engineering". Do NOT use for: exploratory design, greenfield architecture, or problems where boldness is required — use Visionary or First Principles for those. Do NOT us… ([Hyperion-Sports-Tech/claude-skills](https://github.com/Hyperion-Sports-Tech/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pragmatist.md))

- **pragmatist**: Skeptical review of a ranked list of ideas before it ships. Dispatched by the close-gaps skill in Phase 4; also useful alone to stress-test any list of proposed fixes or experiments.
